f(-2) = 0
(a+b)(-2) + 2a + b - 2  = 0 
-2a -2b +2a +b - 2 = 0 
-b - 2 = 0 
b = -2
f(0) = 6
(a-2)(0) + 2a - 2 - 2 = 6
0 + 2a - 2 - 2 = 6
 2a = 10 
a = 5

torej imas funkcijo f(x)= 5x + -2x + 5(3) -2 - 2 ==> 3x + 6

dolocis tocke trikotnika:
T1(0,0)
T2(0,6)
T3(-2,0)
od tega lahko zracunas dolzine stranic
a = 2 
b = 6 
c = dolžina(T2, T3)
c = sqrt((y2-y1)/(x2-x1))
c = sqrt (6/2)
c = sqrt(3)
pravokotni trikotnik, ves da je torej Area = a*b/2 
2*6/2 = 6
Area = 6","",""
